Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on optimization structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 422. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 26 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on optimization structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 422. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 26. QED.

Theorem 215.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
